/* Sparkline Memory Stores */
const SPARKLINE_STORES = {
    'chart-ca-home': { history: [0.90, 0.92, 0.91, 0.93, 0.95], isUp: true },
    'chart-ca-away': { history: [1.05, 1.03, 1.02, 1.00, 0.98], isUp: false },
    'chart-eu-home': { history: [1.80, 1.78, 1.76, 1.75, 1.75], isUp: false },
    'chart-eu-draw': { history: [3.80, 3.85, 3.88, 3.90, 3.90], isUp: true }

let currentMatchKey = 'sin-tha';
let currentLeagueFilter = 'all';
let currentStatusFilter = 'all'; // 'all' | 'live' | 'today' | 'early'
let currentViewGroup = 'league';  // 'league' | 'time'
let currentLineMode = 'double';   // 'double' | 'single'
let currentSearchQuery = '';
const collapsedLeagues = new Set();
const favoriteMatches = new Set();

let isMatchesTableExpanded = false;
const DEFAULT_VISIBLE_MATCH_LIMIT = 4;

/* =============================================
   2. INITIALIZATION (WORDPRESS & STANDALONE COMPATIBLE)
   ============================================= */
async function initNexusOddsApp() {
    const cfg = window.NEXUS_ODDS_CONFIG || {};
    if (cfg.bgImageUrl) {
        const bgEl = document.getElementById('nexus-stadium-bg') || document.querySelector('.banner-bg');
        if (bgEl) bgEl.style.backgroundImage = `url('${cfg.bgImageUrl}')`;
    }
    if (cfg.bannerImageUrl) {
        const bannerImg = document.getElementById('nexus-sidebar-banner-img') || document.querySelector('.sidebar-banner-img');
        if (bannerImg) bannerImg.src = cfg.bannerImageUrl;
    }

    if (window.NexusOdds && window.NexusOdds.adapter) {
        await window.NexusOdds.adapter.init(DEFAULT_MATCH_DATABASE);
        setupDataStoreListeners();
    } else {
        renderMatchesTable('all');
    }

    setupSportsbookControls();
    renderDynamicLeagueTabs();
    setupViewSwitching();
    setupTabs();
    setupNavItems();
    setupSearchFunctionality();
    setupInteractiveOddsGrid();
    setupArticleTocNavigation();
    setupDonutHoverSweep();
    setupPowerCompTabs();
    setupNhacaiAutoScroll();

    const defaultLeague = (cfg.defaultLeague && cfg.defaultLeague !== 'all') ? cfg.defaultLeague : 'all';
    renderMatchesTable(defaultLeague);
    updateStatusCounters();

    checkInitialViewState();
}

if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initNexusOddsApp);
} else {
    initNexusOddsApp();
}

/* =============================================
   3. DATA STORE EVENT SUBSCRIPTIONS (REACTIVE UI)
   ============================================= */
function setupDataStoreListeners() {
    if (!window.NexusOdds) return;

    window.NexusOdds.on('matches:loaded', () => {
        renderDynamicLeagueTabs();
        renderMatchesTable(currentLeagueFilter, currentSearchQuery, currentStatusFilter);
        updateStatusCounters();
        if (currentMatchKey) populateMatchData(currentMatchKey);
    });

    window.NexusOdds.on('odds:updated', ({ key, currentOdds, previousOdds }) => {
        updateTableRowOdds(key, currentOdds, previousOdds);
        if (key === currentMatchKey) {
            updateDetailOddsLive(currentOdds, previousOdds);
        }
    });

    window.NexusOdds.on('match:updated', ({ key }) => {
        if (key === currentMatchKey) populateMatchData(key);
    });
}

/* =============================================
   4. ASIAN SPORTSBOOK TABLE RENDERER
   ============================================= */
function renderMatchesTable(leagueFilter = currentLeagueFilter, searchQuery = currentSearchQuery, statusFilter = currentStatusFilter) {
    currentLeagueFilter = leagueFilter;
    currentSearchQuery = searchQuery;
    currentStatusFilter = statusFilter;

    const boardContent = document.getElementById('sportsbook-board-content');
    if (!boardContent) return;

    // Retrieve matches from store or default fallback
    let matchesList = [];
    if (window.NexusOdds && window.NexusOdds.store) {
        matchesList = window.NexusOdds.store.getFilteredMatches(leagueFilter, searchQuery, statusFilter);
    } else {
        matchesList = Object.keys(DEFAULT_MATCH_DATABASE).map(k => ({ ...DEFAULT_MATCH_DATABASE[k], _key: k }));
        matchesList = matchesList.filter(m => {
            const matchLeague = (m.league || '').toUpperCase();
            const targetLeague = (leagueFilter || 'all').toUpperCase();
            if (targetLeague !== 'ALL' && !matchLeague.includes(targetLeague)) return false;

            if (statusFilter === 'live' && !m.isLive) return false;
            if (statusFilter === 'today' && (m.isLive || !(m.date || '').toLowerCase().includes('hôm nay'))) return false;
            if (statusFilter === 'early' && (!(m.date || '').toLowerCase().includes('ngày mai') && !(m.date || '').toLowerCase().includes('sớm'))) return false;

            if (!searchQuery || searchQuery.trim() === '') return true;
            const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase().trim();
            return (m.home?.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || (m.away?.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q) || matchLeague.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        });
    }

    if (matchesList.length === 0) {
        boardContent.innerHTML = `
            <div style="text-align:center; padding: 48px 16px; background:#081426; color: #9cb5d1; border-radius: 8px;">
                <div style="font-size: 1.1rem; font-weight:700; color: #fff; margin-bottom:6px;">Không tìm thấy trận đấu phù hợp</div>
                <small style="color:#64748b;">Vui lòng thử tìm với từ khóa khác hoặc bấm chọn "Tất cả"</small>
            </div>
        `;
        return;
    }

    const isAllFilter = (leagueFilter === 'all' || !leagueFilter) && (!searchQuery || searchQuery.trim() === '') && (statusFilter === 'all' || !statusFilter);
    const totalCount = matchesList.length;
    const shouldPaginate = isAllFilter && totalCount > DEFAULT_VISIBLE_MATCH_LIMIT;

    let displayMatches = matchesList;
    if (shouldPaginate && !isMatchesTableExpanded) {
        displayMatches = matchesList.slice(0, DEFAULT_VISIBLE_MATCH_LIMIT);
    }

    // Render grouped by League or grouped by Time
    if (currentViewGroup === 'league') {
        renderGroupedByLeague(boardContent, displayMatches, shouldPaginate, totalCount);
    } else {
        renderGroupedByTime(boardContent, displayMatches, shouldPaginate, totalCount);
    }

    // Apply single / double line class to container
    const container = document.getElementById('sportsbook-board-container');
    if (container) {
        if (currentLineMode === 'single') {
            container.classList.add('single-line-mode');
        } else {
            container.classList.remove('single-line-mode');
        }
    }

    attachMatchEventListeners();
}

/**
 * Render grouped by League (Authentic SBOBET / Bong88 Style)
 */
function renderGroupedByLeague(container, matchesList, shouldPaginate = false, totalCount = 0) {
    // Group matches by league
    const grouped = {};
    matchesList.forEach(m => {
        const lg = m.league || 'BÓNG ĐÁ QUỐC TẾ';
        if (!grouped[lg]) grouped[lg] = [];
        grouped[lg].push(m);
    });

    let html = '';
    Object.keys(grouped).forEach(leagueName => {
        const matches = grouped[leagueName];
        const isCollapsed = collapsedLeagues.has(leagueName);

        html += `
        <div class="league-group-block ${isCollapsed ? 'collapsed' : ''}" data-league-name="${leagueName}">
            <div class="league-accordion-header" data-toggle-league="${leagueName}">
                <div class="league-header-left">
                    <span class="league-accordion-icon">▾</span>
                    <span class="league-shield-icon">🛡</span>
                    <span class="league-title-text">${leagueName}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="league-header-right">
                    <span class="league-match-count">${matches.length} trận</span>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="league-table-container">
                <table class="sportsbook-odds-table">
                    <thead>
                        <tr class="sportsbook-market-header-row">
                            <th class="col-match-info">Giờ / Trận Đấu</th>
                            <th class="col-market">Cược Chấp Toàn Trận</th>
                            <th class="col-market">Tài Xỉu Toàn Trận</th>
                            <th class="col-market">1X2 Toàn Trận</th>
                            <th class="col-market">Bàn Thắng Tiếp Theo</th>
                            <th class="col-market">Cược Chấp Hiệp 1</th>
                            <th class="col-market">Tài Xỉu Hiệp 1</th>
                            <th class="col-market">1X2 Hiệp 1</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        ${buildMatchRowsHtml(matches)}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
        `;
    });

    if (shouldPaginate) {
        const remainingCount = totalCount - DEFAULT_VISIBLE_MATCH_LIMIT;
        if (!isMatchesTableExpanded) {
            html += `
            <div class="sportsbook-load-more-wrap">
                <button class="btn-sportsbook-load-more" id="btn-toggle-load-more-matches">
                    <span class="load-more-icon">▾</span>
                    <span class="load-more-text">Xem thêm ${remainingCount} trận đấu khác</span>
                    <span class="load-more-badge">+${remainingCount}</span>
                </button>
            </div>
            `;
        } else {
            html += `
            <div class="sportsbook-load-more-wrap">
                <button class="btn-sportsbook-load-more collapsed" id="btn-toggle-load-more-matches">
                    <span class="load-more-icon">▴</span>
                    <span class="load-more-text">Thu gọn bảng tỷ lệ kèo</span>
                </button>
            </div>
            `;
        }
    }

    container.innerHTML = html;
}

/**
 * Render grouped by Kickoff Time
 */
function renderGroupedByTime(container, matchesList, shouldPaginate = false, totalCount = 0) {
    // Sort matches: Live first, then by time string
    const sorted = [...matchesList].sort((a, b) => {
        if (a.isLive && !b.isLive) return -1;
        if (!a.isLive && b.isLive) return 1;
        return (a.time || '').localeCompare(b.time || '');
    });

    // Group by Date/Time Header
    const grouped = {};
    sorted.forEach(m => {
        const timeHeader = m.isLive ? `${m.date || 'Hôm nay'} – 🔴 ĐANG THI ĐẤU TRỰC TIẾP` : `${m.date || 'Hôm nay'} – ${m.time || '20:00'}`;
        if (!grouped[timeHeader]) grouped[timeHeader] = [];
        grouped[timeHeader].push(m);
    });

    let html = '';
    Object.keys(grouped).forEach(timeLabel => {
        const matches = grouped[timeLabel];
        html += `
        <div class="league-group-block">
            <div class="time-group-banner">
                <span class="time-icon">🕒</span>
                <span>${timeLabel} (${matches.length} trận)</span>
            </div>
            <div class="league-table-container">
                <table class="sportsbook-odds-table">
                    <thead>
                        <tr class="sportsbook-market-header-row">
                            <th class="col-match-info">Giờ / Trận Đấu</th>
                            <th class="col-market">Cược Chấp Toàn Trận</th>
                            <th class="col-market">Tài Xỉu Toàn Trận</th>
                            <th class="col-market">1X2 Toàn Trận</th>
                            <th class="col-market">Bàn Thắng Tiếp Theo</th>
                            <th class="col-market">Cược Chấp Hiệp 1</th>
                            <th class="col-market">Tài Xỉu Hiệp 1</th>
                            <th class="col-market">1X2 Hiệp 1</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        ${buildMatchRowsHtml(matches)}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
        `;
    });

    if (shouldPaginate) {
        const remainingCount = totalCount - DEFAULT_VISIBLE_MATCH_LIMIT;
        if (!isMatchesTableExpanded) {
            html += `
            <div class="sportsbook-load-more-wrap">
                <button class="btn-sportsbook-load-more" id="btn-toggle-load-more-matches">
                    <span class="load-more-icon">▾</span>
                    <span class="load-more-text">Xem thêm ${remainingCount} trận đấu khác</span>
                    <span class="load-more-badge">+${remainingCount}</span>
                </button>
            </div>
            `;
        } else {
            html += `
            <div class="sportsbook-load-more-wrap">
                <button class="btn-sportsbook-load-more collapsed" id="btn-toggle-load-more-matches">
                    <span class="load-more-icon">▴</span>
                    <span class="load-more-text">Thu gọn bảng tỷ lệ kèo</span>
                </button>
            </div>
            `;
        }
    }

    container.innerHTML = html;
}

/**
 * Generate 8-column match row & match footer HTML
 */
function buildMatchRowsHtml(matches) {
    return matches.map((m, index) => {
        const key = m.id || m.key || m._key;
        const odds = m.odds || {};
        const ft = odds.fullTime || {};
        const ht = odds.halfTime || {};
        const ca = odds.chauA || {};
        const eu = odds.europe || {};

        const isLive = m.isLive;
        const liveTime = m.liveTime || (isLive ? (m.time || '1H 23\'') : '');
        const timeDisplay = isLive ? `<span class="live-badge-red">${liveTime}</span>` : `<span class="upcoming-badge-time">🕒 ${m.time || '20:00'}</span>`;

        const homeFav = m.favored === 'home';
        const awayFav = m.favored === 'away';

        // FT HDP Odds
        const ftHdpLine = ft.hdp?.line || ca.homeHdc || '0/0.5';
        const ftHomeOdds = ft.hdp?.homeOdds || ca.homeOdds || '0.90';
        const ftAwayOdds = ft.hdp?.awayOdds || ca.awayOdds || '0.98';
        const ftHdpExtra = ft.extra?.hdp || { line: '0.5', homeOdds: '0.70', awayOdds: '-0.90' };

        // FT O/U Odds
        const ftOuLine = ft.ou?.line || ca.ou?.line || '2.5';
        const ftOverOdds = ft.ou?.overOdds || ca.ou?.over || '0.94';
        const ftUnderOdds = ft.ou?.underOdds || ca.ou?.under || '0.84';
        const ftOuExtra = ft.extra?.ou || { line: '2.5/3', overOdds: '-0.83', underOdds: '0.61' };

        // FT 1X2
        const ftEuHome = ft['1x2']?.home || eu.home || '1.85';
        const ftEuAway = ft['1x2']?.away || eu.away || '3.90';
        const ftEuDraw = ft['1x2']?.draw || eu.draw || '3.50';

        // Next Goal
        const ngHome = ft.nextGoal?.home || '3.00';
        const ngAway = ft.nextGoal?.away || '1.91';
        const ngNone = ft.nextGoal?.none || '3.85';

        // 1H HDP Odds
        const htHdpLine = ht.hdp?.line || '0';
        const htHomeOdds = ht.hdp?.homeOdds || '-0.73';
        const htAwayOdds = ht.hdp?.awayOdds || '0.53';
        const htHdpExtra = ht.extra?.hdp || { line: '0/0.5', homeOdds: '0.46', awayOdds: '-0.66' };

        // 1H O/U Odds
        const htOuLine = ht.ou?.line || '1.5';
        const htOverOdds = ht.ou?.overOdds || '-0.84';
        const htUnderOdds = ht.ou?.underOdds || '0.62';
        const htOuExtra = ht.extra?.ou || { line: '1.5/2', overOdds: '-0.57', underOdds: '0.35' };

        // 1H 1X2 Odds
        const htEuHome = ht['1x2']?.home || '48.00';
        const htEuAway = ht['1x2']?.away || '1.13';
        const htEuDraw = ht['1x2']?.draw || '5.20';

        const renderVal = (v, trend) => {
            const num = parseFloat(v);
            const isNeg = num < 0;
            const cls = isNeg ? 'red' : 'blue';
            const arrHtml = trend === 'up' ? '<span class="arr up">▲</span>' : (trend === 'down' ? '<span class="arr down">▼</span>' : '');
            return `<span class="val-odds ${cls}">${v}${arrHtml}</span>`;
        };

        let rowHtml = `
        <!-- MATCH ODDS ROW -->
        <tr class="sportsbook-match-row" data-match="${key}">
            <!-- 1. Match Time & Teams -->
            <td class="cell-teams-meta">
                <div class="match-time-badge-box">
                    ${timeDisplay}
                </div>
                <div class="team-names-list">
                    <div class="team-row home ${homeFav ? 'favored' : ''}">
                        <span class="t-name ${homeFav ? 'red-favored' : ''}">${m.home?.name || 'Đội Nhà'}</span>
                        <span class="t-score ${homeFav ? 'red-favored' : ''}">${isLive && m.homeScore !== null ? m.homeScore : ''}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="team-row away ${awayFav ? 'favored' : ''}">
                        <span class="t-name ${awayFav ? 'red-favored' : ''}">${m.away?.name || 'Đội Khách'}</span>
                        <span class="t-score ${awayFav ? 'red-favored' : ''}">${isLive && m.awayScore !== null ? m.awayScore : ''}</span>
                    </div>
                    
                </div>
            </td>

            <!-- 2. Cược Chấp Toàn Trận -->
            <td class="cell-odds-market">
                <div class="odds-lines-stack">
                    <div class="odds-sub-box" id="odd-fthdp-h-${key}">
                        ${homeFav ? `<span class="val-line">${ftHdpLine}</span>` : ''}
                        ${renderVal(ftHomeOdds, ft.hdp?.homeTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box" id="odd-fthdp-a-${key}">
                        ${awayFav ? `<span class="val-line">${ftHdpLine}</span>` : ''}
                        ${renderVal(ftAwayOdds, ft.hdp?.awayTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        ${homeFav ? `<span class="val-line">${ftHdpExtra.line}</span>` : ''}
                        ${renderVal(ftHdpExtra.homeOdds, ftHdpExtra.homeTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        ${awayFav ? `<span class="val-line">${ftHdpExtra.line}</span>` : ''}
                        ${renderVal(ftHdpExtra.awayOdds, ftHdpExtra.awayTrend)}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </td>

            <!-- 3. Tài Xỉu Toàn Trận -->
            <td class="cell-odds-market">
                <div class="odds-lines-stack">
                    <div class="odds-sub-box" id="odd-ftou-o-${key}">
                        <span class="val-line">${ftOuLine}</span>
                        ${renderVal(ftOverOdds, ft.ou?.overTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box" id="odd-ftou-u-${key}">
                        <span class="val-tag">u</span>
                        ${renderVal(ftUnderOdds, ft.ou?.underTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        <span class="val-line">${ftOuExtra.line}</span>
                        ${renderVal(ftOuExtra.overOdds, ftOuExtra.overTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        <span class="val-tag">u</span>
                        ${renderVal(ftOuExtra.underOdds, ftOuExtra.underTrend)}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </td>

            <!-- 4. 1X2 Toàn Trận -->
            <td class="cell-odds-market">
                <div class="odds-lines-stack">
                    <div class="odds-sub-box" id="odd-ft1x2-1-${key}">${renderVal(ftEuHome, ft['1x2']?.homeTrend)}</div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box" id="odd-ft1x2-2-${key}">${renderVal(ftEuAway, ft['1x2']?.awayTrend)}</div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box" id="odd-ft1x2-x-${key}">${renderVal(ftEuDraw, ft['1x2']?.drawTrend)}</div>
                </div>
            </td>

            <!-- 5. Bàn Thắng Tiếp Theo -->
            <td class="cell-odds-market">
                <div class="odds-lines-stack">
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        <span class="val-label">Đội Nhà</span>
                        ${renderVal(ngHome, ft.nextGoal?.homeTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        <span class="val-label">Đội Khách</span>
                        ${renderVal(ngAway, ft.nextGoal?.awayTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        <span class="val-label">Không có</span>
                        ${renderVal(ngNone, ft.nextGoal?.noneTrend)}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </td>

            <!-- 6. Cược Chấp Hiệp 1 -->
            <td class="cell-odds-market">
                <div class="odds-lines-stack">
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        ${homeFav ? `<span class="val-line">${htHdpLine}</span>` : ''}
                        ${renderVal(htHomeOdds, ht.hdp?.homeTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        ${awayFav ? `<span class="val-line">${htHdpLine}</span>` : ''}
                        ${renderVal(htAwayOdds, ht.hdp?.awayTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        ${homeFav ? `<span class="val-line">${htHdpExtra.line}</span>` : ''}
                        ${renderVal(htHdpExtra.homeOdds, htHdpExtra.homeTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        ${awayFav ? `<span class="val-line">${htHdpExtra.line}</span>` : ''}
                        ${renderVal(htHdpExtra.awayOdds, htHdpExtra.awayTrend)}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </td>

            <!-- 7. Tài Xỉu Hiệp 1 -->
            <td class="cell-odds-market">
                <div class="odds-lines-stack">
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        <span class="val-line">${htOuLine}</span>
                        ${renderVal(htOverOdds, ht.ou?.overTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        <span class="val-tag">u</span>
                        ${renderVal(htUnderOdds, ht.ou?.underTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        <span class="val-line">${htOuExtra.line}</span>
                        ${renderVal(htOuExtra.overOdds, htOuExtra.overTrend)}
                    </div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">
                        <span class="val-tag">u</span>
                        ${renderVal(htOuExtra.underOdds, htOuExtra.underTrend)}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </td>

            <!-- 8. 1X2 Hiệp 1 -->
            <td class="cell-odds-market">
                <div class="odds-lines-stack">
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">${renderVal(htEuHome, ht['1x2']?.homeTrend)}</div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">${renderVal(htEuAway, ht['1x2']?.awayTrend)}</div>
                    <div class="odds-sub-box">${renderVal(htEuDraw, ht['1x2']?.drawTrend)}</div>
                </div>
            </td>
        </tr>

        <!-- MATCH ACTION FOOTER ROW -->
        <tr class="sportsbook-match-footer-row" data-match="${key}">
            <td colspan="8">
                <div class="match-footer-inner">
                    <button class="btn-view-match-detail" data-action="detail" data-match="${key}">
                        <span class="btn-cta-text">Thông tin chi tiết</span>
                        <svg class="btn-cta-arrow" width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2"><path d="M5 12h14M12 5l7 7-7 7"/></svg>
                    </button>
                </div>
            </td>
        </tr>
        `;

        // IN-TABLE ADVERTISEMENT BANNER (EVERY 2 MATCHES)
        if ((index + 1) % 2 === 0) {
            const adImg = (window.NEXUS_WP_CONFIG && window.NEXUS_WP_CONFIG.adBannerUrl) || '/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/ad_banner.webp';
            const adLink = (window.NEXUS_WP_CONFIG && window.NEXUS_WP_CONFIG.adBannerLink) || '#link-hau-dai';
            rowHtml += `
            <tr class="sportsbook-ad-banner-row">
                <td colspan="8">
                    <div class="sportsbook-ad-banner-container">
                        <a href="${adLink}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="sportsbook-ad-banner-link">
                            <img src="${adImg}" class="sportsbook-ad-banner-img">
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </td>
            </tr>
            `;
        }

        return rowHtml;
    }).join('');
}

/**
 * Attach UI listeners to match rows, accordion headers, star buttons, odds boxes
 */
function attachMatchEventListeners() {
    // 1. Accordion collapse/expand for leagues
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-toggle-league]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const leagueName = btn.dataset.toggleLeague;
            const parent = btn.closest('.league-group-block');
            if (parent) {
                parent.classList.toggle('collapsed');
                if (parent.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
                    collapsedLeagues.add(leagueName);
                } else {
                    collapsedLeagues.delete(leagueName);
                }
            }
        });
    });

    // 2. Team name / Meta click / Detail buttons -> Open match detail
    document.querySelectorAll('.cell-teams-meta, [data-action="detail"]').forEach(el => {
        el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const matchRow = el.closest('[data-match]');
            const key = el.dataset.match || (matchRow ? matchRow.dataset.match : null);
            if (key) showMatchDetailView(key);
        });
    });

    // 4. Odds Sub-box interactive click
    document.querySelectorAll('.odds-sub-box').forEach(box => {
        box.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            box.classList.toggle('active-bet');
        });
    });

    // 5. Load more / Collapse toggle button click
    const btnLoadMore = document.getElementById('btn-toggle-load-more-matches');
    if (btnLoadMore) {
        btnLoadMore.addEventListener('click', () => {
            isMatchesTableExpanded = !isMatchesTableExpanded;
            renderMatchesTable(currentLeagueFilter, currentSearchQuery, currentStatusFilter);
            if (!isMatchesTableExpanded) {
                const tableTop = document.getElementById('sportsbook-board-container');
                if (tableTop) tableTop.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
            }
        });
    }
}

/**
 * Setup toolbar controls (Status Tabs, View Group Toggle)
 */
function setupSportsbookControls() {
    // 1. Status Filter Tabs (Tất cả, Trực tiếp, Hôm nay, Sớm)
    document.querySelectorAll('.status-tab').forEach(tab => {
        tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
            document.querySelectorAll('.status-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
            tab.classList.add('active');
            currentStatusFilter = tab.dataset.status;
            renderMatchesTable(currentLeagueFilter, currentSearchQuery, currentStatusFilter);
        });
    });

    // 2. Group Toggle (Theo Giải Đấu vs Theo Thời Gian)
    const btnLeague = document.getElementById('btn-group-league');
    const btnTime = document.getElementById('btn-group-time');

    if (btnLeague && btnTime) {
        btnLeague.addEventListener('click', () => {
            btnLeague.classList.add('active');
            btnTime.classList.remove('active');
            currentViewGroup = 'league';
            renderMatchesTable(currentLeagueFilter, currentSearchQuery, currentStatusFilter);
        });

        btnTime.addEventListener('click', () => {
            btnTime.classList.add('active');
            btnLeague.classList.remove('active');
            currentViewGroup = 'time';
            renderMatchesTable(currentLeagueFilter, currentSearchQuery, currentStatusFilter);
        });
    }
}

/**
 * Real-time row odds updater with flash indicator
 */
function updateTableRowOdds(matchKey, currentOdds, previousOdds) {
    if (!currentOdds || !currentOdds.fullTime) return;

    const ft = currentOdds.fullTime;
    const ftHdpH = document.getElementById(`odd-fthdp-h-${matchKey}`);
    const ftHdpA = document.getElementById(`odd-fthdp-a-${matchKey}`);

    if (ftHdpH && ft.hdp?.homeOdds) {
        const isUp = previousOdds?.fullTime?.hdp ? parseFloat(ft.hdp.homeOdds) >= parseFloat(previousOdds.fullTime.hdp.homeOdds) : true;
        ftHdpH.classList.remove('flash-up', 'flash-down');
        void ftHdpH.offsetWidth; // Trigger reflow
        ftHdpH.classList.add(isUp ? 'flash-up' : 'flash-down');
    }

    if (ftHdpA && ft.hdp?.awayOdds) {
        const isUp = previousOdds?.fullTime?.hdp ? parseFloat(ft.hdp.awayOdds) >= parseFloat(previousOdds.fullTime.hdp.awayOdds) : false;
        ftHdpA.classList.remove('flash-up', 'flash-down');
        void ftHdpA.offsetWidth;
        ftHdpA.classList.add(isUp ? 'flash-up' : 'flash-down');
    }
}

/**
 * Update Status Counter Badges in Toolbar dynamically
 */
function updateStatusCounters() {
    const list = window.NexusOdds && window.NexusOdds.store
        ? window.NexusOdds.store.getMatchesList()
        : Object.values(DEFAULT_MATCH_DATABASE);

    let all = list.length;
    let live = list.filter(m => m.isLive).length;
    let today = list.filter(m => !m.isLive && (m.date || '').toLowerCase().includes('hôm nay')).length;
    let early = list.filter(m => (m.date || '').toLowerCase().includes('ngày mai') || (m.date || '').toLowerCase().includes('sớm')).length;

    setText('count-all', all);
    setText('count-live', live);
    setText('count-today', today);
    setText('count-early', early);
}

/**
 * Dynamically generate League Filter Tabs based on currently loaded API/Store data
 */
function renderDynamicLeagueTabs() {
    const wrap = document.getElementById('league-tabs-scroll-wrap');
    if (!wrap) return;

    let counts = { all: 0 };
    let leaguesList = [];

    if (window.NexusOdds && window.NexusOdds.store) {
        counts = window.NexusOdds.store.getLeagueCounts();
        const matches = window.NexusOdds.store.getMatchesList();
        const seen = new Set();
        matches.forEach(m => {
            const lg = m.league || 'BÓNG ĐÁ QUỐC TẾ';
            if (!seen.has(lg)) {
                seen.add(lg);
                leaguesList.push(lg);
            }
        });
    } else {
        const matches = Object.values(DEFAULT_MATCH_DATABASE);
        const seen = new Set();
        matches.forEach(m => {
            counts.all++;
            const lg = m.league || 'Khác';
            counts[lg] = (counts[lg] || 0) + 1;
            if (!seen.has(lg)) {
                seen.add(lg);
                leaguesList.push(lg);
            }
        });
    }

    let html = `
        <button class="league-tab ${currentLeagueFilter === 'all' ? 'active' : ''}" data-league="all">
            Tất cả (${counts.all || 0})
        </button>
    `;

    leaguesList.forEach(lg => {
        const cnt = counts[lg] || 0;
        const shortName = lg;
        const isActive = currentLeagueFilter.toUpperCase() === lg.toUpperCase();
        html += `
            <button class="league-tab ${isActive ? 'active' : ''}" data-league="${lg}">
                ${shortName} (${cnt})
            </button>
        `;
    });

    wrap.innerHTML = html;

    // Attach click listeners to dynamically created league tabs
    wrap.querySelectorAll('.league-tab').forEach(tab => {
        tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
            wrap.querySelectorAll('.league-tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
            tab.classList.add('active');
            currentLeagueFilter = tab.dataset.league;
            renderMatchesTable(currentLeagueFilter, currentSearchQuery, currentStatusFilter);
        });
    });
}


/* =============================================
   5. VIEW SWITCHING (TRANG CHỦ VS CHI TIẾT TRẬN ĐẤU)
   ============================================= */
function showHomepageView(updateUrl = true) {
    const homeView = document.getElementById('homepage-matches-view');
    const detailView = document.getElementById('match-detail-view');
    if (homeView) homeView.style.display = 'block';
    if (detailView) detailView.style.display = 'none';

    // Update active state in top navigation
    const topLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.top-link');
    topLinks.forEach(l => {
        if (l.textContent.trim() === 'Trang chủ') l.classList.add('active');
        else if (l.textContent.trim() === 'Soi kèo') l.classList.remove('active');
    });

    // Save state to sessionStorage
    try {
        sessionStorage.setItem('nexus_active_view', 'home');
        sessionStorage.removeItem('nexus_active_match');
    } catch (e) {}

    // Update URL hash without reload
    if (updateUrl && window.location.hash) {
        history.replaceState(null, '', window.location.pathname + window.location.search);
    }

    const mainEl = document.querySelector('.main-content');
    if (mainEl) mainEl.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
}

function showMatchDetailView(matchKey, updateUrl = true) {
    const key = matchKey || currentMatchKey || 'man-liv';
    const matchData = getMatchSafe(key);
    if (!matchData) return;

    currentMatchKey = key;

    const homeView = document.getElementById('homepage-matches-view');
    const detailView = document.getElementById('match-detail-view');
    if (homeView) homeView.style.display = 'none';
    if (detailView) detailView.style.display = 'block';

    // Update breadcrumb
    setText('detail-match-title', `${matchData.home.name} vs ${matchData.away.name}`);

    // Populate data for selected match
    populateMatchData(key);

    // Trigger animations & chart redraws
    triggerDonutSweepAnimation();
    updatePowerComparisonPanel(key, CURRENT_POWER_TAB);
    drawMiniCharts();
    renderCommunityForms(key);

    // Update active state in top nav (Keep Trang chủ active as requested)
    const topLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.top-link');
    topLinks.forEach(l => {
        if (l.textContent.trim() === 'Trang chủ') l.classList.add('active');
        else l.classList.remove('active');
    });

    // Save state to sessionStorage
    try {
        sessionStorage.setItem('nexus_active_view', 'detail');
        sessionStorage.setItem('nexus_active_match', key);
    } catch (e) {}

    // Update URL hash for F5 and bookmark support
    if (updateUrl) {
        try {
            history.replaceState(null, '', '#match-' + key);
        } catch (e) {
            window.location.hash = 'match-' + key;
        }
    }

    const mainEl = document.querySelector('.main-content');
    if (mainEl) mainEl.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
}

/**
 * Check initial view on page load / F5 refresh
 */
function checkInitialViewState() {
    let targetMatch = null;

    // 1. Check URL Hash (e.g. #match-man-liv or #man-liv)
    const hash = window.location.hash.replace(/^#/, '').trim();
    if (hash.startsWith('match-')) {
        const hKey = hash.replace(/^match-/, '');
        if (getMatchSafe(hKey)) targetMatch = hKey;
    } else if (hash && getMatchSafe(hash)) {
        targetMatch = hash;
    }

    // 2. Check URL Search Query Param (e.g. ?match=man-liv)
    if (!targetMatch) {
        try {
            const urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const pKey = urlParams.get('match');
            if (pKey && getMatchSafe(pKey)) targetMatch = pKey;
        } catch (e) {}
    }

    // 3. Check sessionStorage (persists during F5 within the same tab)
    if (!targetMatch) {
        try {
            const sessionView = sessionStorage.getItem('nexus_active_view');
            const sessionMatch = sessionStorage.getItem('nexus_active_match');
            if (sessionView === 'detail' && sessionMatch && getMatchSafe(sessionMatch)) {
                targetMatch = sessionMatch;
            }
        } catch (e) {}
    }

    if (targetMatch) {
        showMatchDetailView(targetMatch, false);
    } else {
        showHomepageView(false);
    }
}

function setupViewSwitching() {
    const btnBack = document.getElementById('btn-back-to-list');
    if (btnBack) {
        btnBack.addEventListener('click', () => showHomepageView(true));
    }

    const bcHome = document.getElementById('bc-home');
    if (bcHome) {
        bcHome.addEventListener('click', () => showHomepageView(true));
    }

    // Listen to browser Back / Forward buttons & Hash changes
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', () => {
        const hash = window.location.hash.replace(/^#/, '').trim();
        if (hash.startsWith('match-')) {
            const mKey = hash.replace(/^match-/, '');
            if (getMatchSafe(mKey)) showMatchDetailView(mKey, false);
        } else if (!hash || hash === 'home') {
            showHomepageView(false);
        }
    });

    window.addEventListener('popstate', () => {
        checkInitialViewState();
    });
}


/* =============================================
   6. POPULATE MATCH DETAIL DATA
   ============================================= */
function getMatchSafe(key) {
    if (window.NexusOdds && window.NexusOdds.store) {
        const m = window.NexusOdds.store.getMatch(key);
        if (m) return m;
    }
    return DEFAULT_MATCH_DATABASE[key] || DEFAULT_MATCH_DATABASE['man-liv'];
}

function populateMatchData(matchKey = 'man-liv') {
    const d = getMatchSafe(matchKey);
    if (!d) return;

    setText('home-team-name', d.home?.name || 'ĐỘI NHÀ');
    setText('away-team-name', d.away?.name || 'ĐỘI KHÁCH');
    setText('match-time', d.time || '20:00');
    setText('league-name', d.league || 'BÓNG ĐÁ');

    // Populate Dynamic Lineup & Pitch Formation Card
    updateLineupPitchData(matchKey);

    // Populate Technical Statistics
    if (d.techStats) {
        const ts = d.techStats;
        updateTechStatRow('goals', ts.goals?.h || 0, ts.goals?.a || 0, false);
        updateTechStatRow('corners', ts.corners?.h || 0, ts.corners?.a || 0, false);
        updateTechStatRow('yellow', ts.yellow?.h || 0, ts.yellow?.a || 0, false);
        updateTechStatRow('sot', ts.sot?.h || 0, ts.sot?.a || 0, false);
        updateTechStatRow('soff', ts.soff?.h || 0, ts.soff?.a || 0, false);
        updateTechStatRow('att', ts.att?.h || 0, ts.att?.a || 0, false);
        updateTechStatRow('datt', ts.datt?.h || 0, ts.datt?.a || 0, false);
        updateTechStatRow('pos', ts.possession?.h || 50, ts.possession?.a || 50, true);
        updateTechStatRow('shots', ts.shots?.h || 0, ts.shots?.a || 0, false);
    }

    // Odds - Team Tags & Labels
    setText('ca-home-tag', d.home?.name || 'ĐỘI NHÀ');
    setText('ca-away-tag', d.away?.name || 'ĐỘI KHÁCH');
    setText('ca-home-tag-h1', d.home?.name || 'ĐỘI NHÀ');
    setText('ca-away-tag-h1', d.away?.name || 'ĐỘI KHÁCH');
    setText('eu-home-tag', `${d.home?.name || 'ĐỘI NHÀ'} (1)`);
    setText('eu-away-tag', `${d.away?.name || 'ĐỘI KHÁCH'} (2)`);

    // Odds - Châu Á & Tài Xỉu
    const odds = d.odds || {};
    const ca = odds.chauA || {};
    const ou = ca.ou || {};
    const eu = odds.europe || {};

    setText('ca-home-hdc', ca.homeHdc || '-0.50');
    setText('ca-home-odds', (ca.homeOdds || '0.92') + ' ↑');
    setText('ca-away-hdc', ca.awayHdc || '+0.50');
    setText('ca-away-odds', (ca.awayOdds || '0.98') + ' ↓');
    setText('ou-over', (ou.over || '0.90') + ' ↑');
    setText('ou-under', (ou.under || '0.98') + ' ↓');

    // Reset sparkline history for new match
    const baseHome = parseFloat(ca.homeOdds) || 0.95;
    const baseAway = parseFloat(ca.awayOdds) || 0.98;
    SPARKLINE_STORES['chart-ca-home'] = { history: [baseHome - 0.03, baseHome - 0.02, baseHome - 0.01, baseHome], isUp: true };
    SPARKLINE_STORES['chart-ca-away'] = { history: [baseAway + 0.03, baseAway + 0.02, baseAway + 0.01, baseAway], isUp: false };

    // Odds - Châu Âu
    setText('eu-home', eu.home || '1.85');
    setText('eu-draw', eu.draw || '3.50');
    setText('eu-away', eu.away || '3.90');

    // Prediction
    const pred = d.prediction || { home: 50, draw: 25, away: 25 };
    setText('pred-home-pct', `${d.home?.name || 'Đội nhà'} ${pred.home}%`);
    setText('pred-draw-pct', `Hòa ${pred.draw}%`);
    setText('pred-away-pct', `${d.away?.name || 'Đội khách'} ${pred.away}%`);
    setText('donut-main-pct', pred.home + '%');
    setText('donut-main-team', d.home?.name || 'Đội nhà');

    // Community Status & Form
    renderCommunityForms(matchKey);
}

/**
 * Real-time update for match detail view odds
 */
function updateDetailOddsLive(currentOdds, previousOdds) {
    if (!currentOdds) return;
    const ca = currentOdds.chauA || {};
    const ou = ca.ou || {};
    const eu = currentOdds.europe || {};

    const elHome = document.getElementById('ca-home-odds');
    const elAway = document.getElementById('ca-away-odds');
    const elOver = document.getElementById('ou-over');
    const elUnder = document.getElementById('ou-under');

    if (elHome && ca.homeOdds) {
        const isUp = previousOdds?.chauA ? parseFloat(ca.homeOdds) >= parseFloat(previousOdds.chauA.homeOdds) : true;
        elHome.textContent = `${ca.homeOdds} ${isUp ? '↑' : '↓'}`;
        elHome.className = `odds-val ${isUp ? 'up' : 'down'}`;

        SPARKLINE_STORES['chart-ca-home'].history.push(parseFloat(ca.homeOdds));
        if (SPARKLINE_STORES['chart-ca-home'].history.length > 8) SPARKLINE_STORES['chart-ca-home'].history.shift();
        SPARKLINE_STORES['chart-ca-home'].isUp = isUp;
    }

    if (elAway && ca.awayOdds) {
        const isUp = previousOdds?.chauA ? parseFloat(ca.awayOdds) >= parseFloat(previousOdds.chauA.awayOdds) : false;
        elAway.textContent = `${ca.awayOdds} ${isUp ? '↑' : '↓'}`;
        elAway.className = `odds-val ${isUp ? 'up' : 'down'}`;

        SPARKLINE_STORES['chart-ca-away'].history.push(parseFloat(ca.awayOdds));
        if (SPARKLINE_STORES['chart-ca-away'].history.length > 8) SPARKLINE_STORES['chart-ca-away'].history.shift();
        SPARKLINE_STORES['chart-ca-away'].isUp = isUp;
    }

    if (elOver && ou.over) {
        const isUp = previousOdds?.chauA?.ou ? parseFloat(ou.over) >= parseFloat(previousOdds.chauA.ou.over) : true;
        elOver.textContent = `${ou.over} ${isUp ? '↑' : '↓'}`;
        elOver.className = `odds-val ${isUp ? 'up' : 'down'}`;
    }

    if (elUnder && ou.under) {
        const isUp = previousOdds?.chauA?.ou ? parseFloat(ou.under) >= parseFloat(previousOdds.chauA.ou.under) : false;
        elUnder.textContent = `${ou.under} ${isUp ? '↑' : '↓'}`;
        elUnder.className = `odds-val ${isUp ? 'up' : 'down'}`;
    }

    if (eu.home) setText('eu-home', eu.home);
    if (eu.draw) setText('eu-draw', eu.draw);
    if (eu.away) setText('eu-away', eu.away);

    drawMiniCharts();
}


/* =============================================
   7. TECHNICAL STATISTICS ANIMATED BARS
   ============================================= */
function updateTechStatRow(key, homeVal, awayVal, isPercentage = false) {
    const elHomeText = document.getElementById(`ts-${key}-h`);
    const elAwayText = document.getElementById(`ts-${key}-a`);
    const elHomeBar = document.getElementById(`ts-bar-${key}-h`);
    const elAwayBar = document.getElementById(`ts-bar-${key}-a`);

    const valSuffix = isPercentage ? '%' : '';
    const sum = (homeVal + awayVal) || 1;
    const hPct = Math.round((homeVal / sum) * 100);
    const aPct = Math.round((awayVal / sum) * 100);

    const targetHPct = Math.max(8, hPct);
    const targetAPct = Math.max(8, aPct);

    if (elHomeBar && elHomeBar._animId) cancelAnimationFrame(elHomeBar._animId);

    // Initial reset to 0
    if (elHomeText) elHomeText.textContent = '0' + valSuffix;
    if (elAwayText) elAwayText.textContent = '0' + valSuffix;
    if (elHomeBar) elHomeBar.style.width = '0%';
    if (elAwayBar) elAwayBar.style.width = '0%';

    const duration = 950;
    let startTime = null;

    function animateStep(currentTime) {
        if (!startTime) startTime = currentTime;
        const elapsed = currentTime - startTime;
        const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const ease = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);

        const currentHome = Math.round(homeVal * ease);
        const currentAway = Math.round(awayVal * ease);

        if (elHomeText) elHomeText.textContent = currentHome + valSuffix;
        if (elAwayText) elAwayText.textContent = currentAway + valSuffix;
        if (elHomeBar) elHomeBar.style.width = (targetHPct * ease) + '%';
        if (elAwayBar) elAwayBar.style.width = (targetAPct * ease) + '%';

        if (progress < 1) {
            const animId = requestAnimationFrame(animateStep);
            if (elHomeBar) elHomeBar._animId = animId;
        } else {
            if (elHomeText) elHomeText.textContent = homeVal + valSuffix;
            if (elAwayText) elAwayText.textContent = awayVal + valSuffix;
            if (elHomeBar) elHomeBar.style.width = targetHPct + '%';
            if (elAwayBar) elAwayBar.style.width = targetAPct + '%';
        }
    }

    requestAnimationFrame(animateStep);
}

function setText(id, val) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.textContent = val;
}

function setStyle(id, prop, val) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.style[prop] = val;
}


/* =============================================
   8. PREDICTION DONUT CHART (HOVER SWEEP)
   ============================================= */
function drawDonutChart(progress = 1) {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('donut-chart');
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const W = canvas.width;
    const H = canvas.height;
    const cx = W / 2, cy = H / 2;
    const r = (W / 2) - 8;
    const innerR = r - 14;

    const d = getMatchSafe(currentMatchKey);
    const pred = d?.prediction || { home: 50, draw: 25, away: 25 };

    const slices = [
        { val: pred.home, color: '#6CB4E4', glow: 'rgba(108,180,228,0.8)' },
        { val: pred.draw, color: '#f5a623', glow: 'rgba(245,166,35,0.8)' },
        { val: pred.away, color: '#C8102E', glow: 'rgba(200,16,46,0.8)' }
    ];

    const total = slices.reduce((s, sl) => s + sl.val, 0) || 100;
    const startAngle = -Math.PI / 2;

    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, r + 5, startAngle, startAngle + Math.PI * 2 * progress);
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#00d4ff';
    ctx.lineWidth = 3.5;
    ctx.shadowColor = '#00d4ff';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();

    let currentStart = startAngle;
    slices.forEach((sl) => {
        const sliceAngle = (sl.val / total) * Math.PI * 2 * progress;
        if (sliceAngle > 0.001) {
            ctx.save();
            ctx.beginPath();
            ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
            ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, currentStart, currentStart + sliceAngle);
            ctx.closePath();
            ctx.fillStyle = sl.color;
            ctx.shadowColor = sl.glow;
            ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#060d1a';
            ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
            ctx.stroke();
            ctx.restore();
        }
        currentStart += sliceAngle;
    });

    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(cx, cy, innerR, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#060f20';
    ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,212,255,0.5)';
    ctx.shadowBlur = 12;
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
}

function setupDonutHoverSweep() {
    const card = document.querySelector('.ai-pred-card');
    const wrapper = document.querySelector('.donut-wrapper');
    const target = wrapper || card;

    if (target) {
        target.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => {
            triggerDonutSweepAnimation();
        });
    }
}

function triggerDonutSweepAnimation() {
    let startTime = null;
    const duration = 1600;
    const match = getMatchSafe(currentMatchKey);
    const targetPct = match?.prediction?.home || 50;

    function step(timestamp) {
        if (!startTime) startTime = timestamp;
        const elapsed = timestamp - startTime;
        const rawProgress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);
        const easeProgress = 1 - Math.pow(1 - rawProgress, 3);

        drawDonutChart(easeProgress);

        const currentPct = Math.round(targetPct * easeProgress);
        setText('donut-main-pct', currentPct + '%');

        if (rawProgress < 1) {
            requestAnimationFrame(step);
        } else {
            drawDonutChart(1);
        }
    }

    requestAnimationFrame(step);
}


/* =============================================
   9. ODDS TABS & INTERACTION
   ============================================= */
function setupTabs() {
    const tabs = document.querySelectorAll('.odds-tab');
    const contents = document.querySelectorAll('.odds-content');

    tabs.forEach(tab => {
        tab.addEventListener('click', () => {
            tabs.forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
            contents.forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));

            tab.classList.add('active');
            const id = 'content-' + tab.dataset.tab;
            const content = document.getElementById(id);
            if (content) {
                content.classList.add('active');
                requestAnimationFrame(drawMiniCharts);
            }
        });
    });
}

function setupInteractiveOddsGrid() {
    const oddsVals = document.querySelectorAll('.odds-val');
    oddsVals.forEach(val => {
        val.addEventListener('click', () => {
            oddsVals.forEach(v => v.style.outline = '');
            val.style.outline = '2px solid #00d4ff';
            val.style.outlineOffset = '2px';
            setTimeout(() => val.style.outline = '', 2000);
        });
    });
}

function setupNavItems() {
    const topLinks = document.querySelectorAll('.top-link');
    topLinks.forEach(link => {
        link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const text = link.textContent.trim();
            if (text === 'Trang chủ') {
                showHomepageView();
            } else if (text === 'Soi kèo') {
                showMatchDetailView();
            } else {
                topLinks.forEach(l => l.classList.remove('active'));
                link.classList.add('active');
            }
        });
    });

    const brandLogo = document.querySelector('.nav-brand');
    if (brandLogo) {
        brandLogo.style.cursor = 'pointer';
        brandLogo.addEventListener('click', showHomepageView);
    }
}


/* =============================================
   10. REAL-TIME SPARKLINE CHARTS
   ============================================= */
function drawMiniCharts() {
    Object.keys(SPARKLINE_STORES).forEach(id => {
        const canvas = document.getElementById(id);
        if (!canvas) return;
        const store = SPARKLINE_STORES[id];
        const color = store.isUp ? '#00e676' : '#ff4d6d';
        drawSparkline(canvas, store.history, color);
    });
}

function drawSparkline(canvas, data, color) {
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const W = canvas.width;
    const H = canvas.height;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

    if (!data || data.length < 2) return;

    const min = Math.min(...data);
    const max = Math.max(...data);
    const range = max - min || 0.05;
    const pad = 4;

    const pts = data.map((val, i) => ({
        x: pad + (i / (data.length - 1)) * (W - pad * 2),
        y: H - pad - ((val - min) / range) * (H - pad * 2)
    }));

    const grad = ctx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H);
    grad.addColorStop(0, color + '55');
    grad.addColorStop(1, color + '00');
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
    pts.slice(1).forEach(p => ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y));
    ctx.lineTo(pts[pts.length - 1].x, H);
    ctx.lineTo(pts[0].x, H);
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle = grad;
    ctx.fill();

    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
    pts.slice(1).forEach(p => ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y));
    ctx.strokeStyle = color;
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.shadowColor = color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 8;
    ctx.stroke();

    const last = pts[pts.length - 1];
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(last.x, last.y, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.fillStyle = color;
    ctx.shadowColor = color;
    ctx.shadowBlur = 10;
    ctx.fill();
}


/* =============================================
   11. POWER COMPARISON PANEL
   ============================================= */
let CURRENT_POWER_MATCH = 'man-liv';
let CURRENT_POWER_TAB = 'h2h';

function updatePowerComparisonPanel(matchKey = 'man-liv', tabKey = 'h2h') {
    CURRENT_POWER_MATCH = matchKey;
    CURRENT_POWER_TAB = tabKey;

    const d = getMatchSafe(matchKey);
    if (!d) return;

    setText('power-home-name', d.home?.name || 'ĐỘI NHÀ');
    setText('power-away-name', d.away?.name || 'ĐỘI KHÁCH');

    const h2hData = {
        title: 'So Sánh Đối Đầu',
        homeScore: 46, awayScore: 54,
        homePct: 50, awayPct: 50,
        diamond: [0.65, 0.80, 0.85, 0.70],
        awayDiamond: [0.75, 0.70, 0.75, 0.82],
        rows: [

function setupPowerCompTabs() {
    const tabBtns = document.querySelectorAll('.ptab-btn');
    tabBtns.forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            tabBtns.forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
            btn.classList.add('active');
            const tabKey = btn.dataset.ptab;
            updatePowerComparisonPanel(CURRENT_POWER_MATCH, tabKey);
        });
    });
}

function drawDiamondRadarChart(homeVals = [0.65, 0.8, 0.85, 0.7], awayVals = [0.75, 0.7, 0.75, 0.82]) {
    const canvas = document.getElementById('diamond-radar-chart');
    if (!canvas) return;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    const W = canvas.width;
    const H = canvas.height;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

    const cx = W / 2;
    const cy = H / 2;
    const maxR = 60;

    const axes = [
        { label: 'Đối đầu', angle: -Math.PI / 2 },
        { label: 'Phong độ', angle: 0 },
        { label: 'Tấn công', angle: Math.PI / 2 },
        { label: 'Phòng thủ', angle: Math.PI }
    ];

    // Grid concentric diamond rings
    [0.3, 0.6, 0.85, 1.0].forEach(level => {
        ctx.beginPath();
        axes.forEach((axis, i) => {
            const r = maxR * level;
            const x = cx + r * Math.cos(axis.angle);
            const y = cy + r * Math.sin(axis.angle);
            if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
            else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        });
        ctx.closePath();
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0, 212, 255, 0.18)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1;
        ctx.stroke();
    });

    // Axes lines & labels
    axes.forEach(axis => {
        const x = cx + maxR * Math.cos(axis.angle);
        const y = cy + maxR * Math.sin(axis.angle);

        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(cx, cy);
        ctx.lineTo(x, y);
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0, 212, 255, 0.22)';
        ctx.stroke();

        ctx.font = '10px Montserrat, sans-serif';
        ctx.fillStyle = '#8daecf';

        const labelOffset = maxR + 14;
        const lx = cx + labelOffset * Math.cos(axis.angle);
        const ly = cy + labelOffset * Math.sin(axis.angle);

        if (axis.angle === -Math.PI / 2) {
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';
        } else if (axis.angle === 0) {
            ctx.textAlign = 'left';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
        } else if (axis.angle === Math.PI / 2) {
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'top';
        } else {
            ctx.textAlign = 'right';
            ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
        }
        ctx.fillText(axis.label, lx, ly);
    });

    // Home Polygon (Orange)
    ctx.beginPath();
    homeVals.forEach((val, i) => {
        const r = maxR * Math.min(1, Math.max(0.2, val));
        const x = cx + r * Math.cos(axes[i].angle);
        const y = cy + r * Math.sin(axes[i].angle);
        if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
        else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    });
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255, 152, 0, 0.35)';
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#ff9800';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.stroke();

    // Away Polygon (Blue)
    ctx.beginPath();
    awayVals.forEach((val, i) => {
        const r = maxR * Math.min(1, Math.max(0.2, val));
        const x = cx + r * Math.cos(axes[i].angle);
        const y = cy + r * Math.sin(axes[i].angle);
        if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y);
        else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    });
    ctx.closePath();
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0, 212, 255, 0.35)';
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#00d4ff';
    ctx.lineWidth = 2;
    ctx.stroke();
}


/* =============================================
   12. COMMUNITY FORM & FORCE NEWS
   ============================================= */
function renderCommunityForms(matchKey = 'man-liv') {
    const d = getMatchSafe(matchKey);
    if (!d) return;

    const mcForm = document.getElementById('mc-form');
    const lvForm = document.getElementById('lv-form');
    const comm = d.community || {};
    const homeComm = comm.mc || { form: ['W', 'D', 'W', 'W', 'W'], status: 'Lực lượng ổn định' };
    const awayComm = comm.lv || { form: ['W', 'L', 'W', 'D', 'W'], status: 'Lực lượng ổn định' };

    if (mcForm && Array.isArray(homeComm.form)) {
        mcForm.innerHTML = homeComm.form.map(f => `<span class="fb ${f.toLowerCase()}">${f}</span>`).join('');
    }
    if (lvForm && Array.isArray(awayComm.form)) {
        lvForm.innerHTML = awayComm.form.map(f => `<span class="fb ${f.toLowerCase()}">${f}</span>`).join('');
    }

    const fnHome = document.getElementById('force-home');
    if (fnHome) {
        const isGood = homeComm.status.includes('Đầy') || homeComm.status.includes('sung') || homeComm.status.includes('lại') || homeComm.status.includes('bàn') || homeComm.status.includes('cao') || homeComm.status.includes('định');
        fnHome.innerHTML = `${d.home?.name || 'Đội nhà'}: <span class="${isGood ? 'highlight-green' : 'highlight-red'}">${homeComm.status}</span>`;
    }

    const fnAway = document.getElementById('force-away');
    if (fnAway) {
        const isBad = awayComm.status.startsWith('Vắng') || awayComm.status.includes('yếu') || awayComm.status.includes('Chấn');
        fnAway.innerHTML = `${d.away?.name || 'Đội khách'}: <span class="${isBad ? 'highlight-red' : 'highlight-green'}">${awayComm.status}</span>`;
    }
}


/* =============================================
   13. SEARCH & TABLE OF CONTENTS NAVIGATION
   ============================================= */
function setupSearchFunctionality() {
    const input = document.getElementById('search-input');
    if (!input) return;

    let debounceTimer = null;
    input.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
        debounceTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            const term = e.target.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            renderMatchesTable(currentLeagueFilter, term);
        }, 150);
    });
}

function setupArticleTocNavigation() {
    const tocItems = document.querySelectorAll('.toc-item');
    tocItems.forEach(item => {
        item.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            const href = item.getAttribute('href');
            if (!href || !href.startsWith('#')) return;
            const targetId = href.substring(1);
            const targetEl = document.getElementById(targetId);
            if (targetEl) {
                tocItems.forEach(i => i.classList.remove('active'));
                item.classList.add('active');
                targetEl.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
            }
        });
    });
}

/* =============================================
   14. PING-PONG AUTO SCROLL (TOP NHÀ CÁI UY TÍN: 1 -> 7 -> 1)
   ============================================= */
function setupNhacaiAutoScroll() {
    const list = document.querySelector('.nhacai-list');
    if (!list) return;

    // Avoid duplicate initialization
    if (list._autoScrollInitialized) return;
    list._autoScrollInitialized = true;

    // Remove any previously cloned items to keep strictly original 1 -> 7 items
    const cloned = list.querySelectorAll('.cloned-item');
    cloned.forEach(el => el.remove());

    let isPaused = false;
    let isBoundaryPaused = false;
    let direction = 1; // 1: cuộn xuống (1 -> 7), -1: cuộn lên (7 -> 1)
    let scrollPos = 0;
    const speed = 0.45; // Tốc độ trôi chậm siêu mượt (pixel mỗi frame)
    let pauseTimer = null;
    let animId = null;

    function getMaxScroll() {
        return Math.max(0, list.scrollHeight - list.clientHeight);
    }

    // Tạm dừng khi rê chuột vào hoặc chạm màn hình
    list.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { isPaused = true; });
    list.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { 
        isPaused = false; 
        scrollPos = list.scrollTop; 
    });
    list.addEventListener('touchstart', () => { isPaused = true; }, { passive: true });
    list.addEventListener('touchend', () => { 
        isPaused = false; 
        scrollPos = list.scrollTop; 
    }, { passive: true });
    list.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        if (isPaused) {
            scrollPos = list.scrollTop;
        }
    });

    function step() {
        if (!isPaused && !isBoundaryPaused) {
            const maxScroll = getMaxScroll();
            if (maxScroll > 0) {
                scrollPos += speed * direction;

                // Khi chạm đáy (nhà cái số 7)
                if (direction === 1 && scrollPos >= maxScroll) {
                    scrollPos = maxScroll;
                    list.scrollTop = scrollPos;
                    direction = -1; // Đổi hướng cuộn ngược lên lại
                    isBoundaryPaused = true;
                    clearTimeout(pauseTimer);
                    pauseTimer = setTimeout(() => { isBoundaryPaused = false; }, 1400); // Dừng lại 1.4 giây để xem
                } 
                // Khi chạm đỉnh (nhà cái số 1)
                else if (direction === -1 && scrollPos <= 0) {
                    scrollPos = 0;
                    list.scrollTop = scrollPos;
                    direction = 1; // Đổi hướng cuộn xuôi xuống lại
                    isBoundaryPaused = true;
                    clearTimeout(pauseTimer);
                    pauseTimer = setTimeout(() => { isBoundaryPaused = false; }, 1400); // Dừng lại 1.4 giây để xem
                } else {
                    list.scrollTop = scrollPos;
                }
            }
        }
        animId = requestAnimationFrame(step);
    }

    animId = requestAnimationFrame(step);
}

function updateLineupPitchData(matchKey) {
    const d = getMatchSafe(matchKey);
    if (!d) return;

    const homeName = d.home?.name || 'Đội nhà';
    const awayName = d.away?.name || 'Đội khách';
    const homeManager = d.home?.manager || 'Đang cập nhật';
    const awayManager = d.away?.manager || 'Đang cập nhật';

    setText('lineup-team-h', homeName);
    setText('lineup-team-a', awayName);
    setText('bench-home-title', `Dự bị ${homeName}`);
    setText('bench-away-title', `Dự bị ${awayName}`);
    setText('bench-home-mgr', `HLV: ${homeManager}`);
    setText('bench-away-mgr', `HLV: ${awayManager}`);

    // Lookup lineup data or create default
    const lineup = MATCH_LINEUP_DATABASE[matchKey] || {
        formH: '4-3-3', formA: '4-3-3',
        homePlayers: [
            { num: 1, name: 'GK', gk: true, pos: [8, 50] },
